Adam implementation:
```
def _adam_step(v, grad, m, v2, t, lr):
    beta1, beta2, eps=0.9,0.999,bias
    m = b1*m + (1-b1)*grad
    v2 = b2*v2 + (1-b2)*grad*grad
    mhat=m/(1-b1**t); vhat=v2/(1-b2**t)
    step = lr*mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+eps)
    v -= step
    v -= v.mean()
    return m,v2,v
```
Memory arrays n. Need update `m`, `v2`. Could use `np.multiply` etc.

Alternative use plain multiplicative update with gradient gl: logp -= lr*gl? Let's maybe compare. We can implement two variants and choose best via internal score. But code complexity.

Soft function:
```
def softmax_from_v(v):
    v = np.asarray(v)
    m = v.max()
    u = np.exp(v-m)
    s = u.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <=0:
        # fallback uniform
        p = np.ones_like(v)/len(v)
        vp = np.full_like(v, -np.log(len(v)))
    else:
        p = u/s
        vp = v - m - np.log(s)  # this is log p
    return p, vp
```
If some u underflow 0, vp -inf; okay. But if v has -inf, m finite, exp(-inf)=0. vp -inf. If many -inf, sum ok. If v all -inf? not.

Optimization function:
```
def run_log_optimizer(p0, n_iters, lr, beta, mode, K, M, time_limit=None, t_end=None, seed=0):
    n=p0.size; N=2n-1
    p = normalize p positive
    v = np.log(p)
    m = zeros; v2=zeros; t=0
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score(p)
    # maybe anneal beta over iterations: beta = beta_start + (beta_end-beta_start)*t/T or topk K
    for it in range(n_iters):
       p, v = softmax(v)
       # compute FFT
       P = rfft(p,M)
       q = irfft(P*P, M)[:N]
       q = np.maximum(q, 0)  # remove tiny negative
       # ensure sum q=1? Due numerical, q.sum maybe 1; can normalize q? Normalizing q changes scores slightly. Better not. But if negative clipped positive, sum might >1. For weights use q_safe.
       smax = q.max()
       score = 2*n*smax
       if score < best_score: best=...
       # compute s weights
       if mode topk: ... else LSE ...
       H = cross using P and srev
       gl = 2*p*H
       # Remove NaN
       if not all finite: break
       dotg = np.dot(p, gl)
       grad = gl - p*dotg
       # if grad norm zero break
       t +=1
       update v Adam
       # step clip
       if it % monitor ==0 print
    return best_score,best_p
```

But if we compute P before updating v, okay.

Weight computation with q from P*P may have q values with sum not exactly 1. We can optionally renormalize q for weights: `q_w = np.maximum(q, 0); qsum=q_w.sum(); if qsum>0: q_w/=qsum` to maintain probability. But true q sum should be p sum squared=1; float errors/can be 0.999999. For log objective, scale of q matters: if sum not 1, max log changes constant? Minimizing max q exactly, scaling q by constant all coefficients changes log objective same (add constant) if uniform? Actually if q sum not 1 due p sum not 1? p sum from softmax exactly 1 (within float). Due FFT roundoff, sum error tiny. Use normalized q for weights and score maybe use exact? For score, use max(q); small error okay. Use q_safe = q if sum positive. If sum differs >1e-8, normalize q for weights? Normalizing q changes log weights but objective should use actual q. p sum should be 1, so qsum ~1. We can normalize q by qsum to ensure weights valid; score can still use smax; normalization tiny.

Let's write helper functions precisely.

```
def _normalize_p(p, floor=1e-15):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    p[p < 0] = 0.0
    p[p > 1000] = 1000  # not needed for p sum
    s = p.sum()
    if s <= 0:
       return np.full(p.shape, 1.0/p.size if p.size>0 else 1.0)
    p = p/s
    if floor>0:
       p = p + floor
       p /= p.sum()
    return p
```
But adding floor changes length; if parent has zeros, okay. Use floor = 1e-15 for n up to 1e5 total 1e-10.

`_score_from_p(p):` Need q. If p size small. Use M = pow2_ge(2*n-1) maybe. But can call with p normalized.
```
def _score_p(p):
  n=p.size; if n==0 return inf
  p=_normalize_p(p)
  M=pow2_ge(2*n-1)
  P=np.fft.rfft(p,M)
  q=np.fft.irfft(P*P, M)[:2*n-1]
  return 2.0*n*float(np.max(q))
```
Could have M next pow of 2n-1. For scoring only.

Pseudo:
```
def run_optimizer(p0, phase_deadline, mode, beta=16.0, beta_end=None, K=64, K_end=None, lr=0.5, label='', max_iter=10_000_000, verbose_every=10.0):
    import time
    n = len(p0)
    N = 2*n-1
    M = 1 << (3*n - 2).bit_length()
    center_fft = np.fft.rfft
    inv_fft = np.fft.irfft
    exp = np.exp
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    p = _normalize_p(p0, floor=1e-15)
    v = np.log(p)
    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v2 = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    best_score = _score_from_p(p)  # maybe use q computed; to save compute initial score outside? Could compute inside first iter and skip? We can compute initial using q after FFT. But before loop need best_score; set inf and compute in loop.
    best_score = np.inf
    best_p = p.copy()
    last_print = t_start
    b1=0.9;b2=0.999;eps=1e-8;max_norm=1.0;small=1e-15
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time.monotonic() > phase_deadline: break
       if beta_end is not None: frac=... else 0
       # stable softmax
       vm = v.max()
       u = exp(v - vm)
       s = u.sum()
       if not finite or s==0: p = uniform; v = log(p); reset? break
       else:
          p = u/s
          # add small floor
          p = p + small
          p = p / p.sum()
          v = np.log(p)  # This recomputes log, cost O(n). Could update v = v-vm-log(s) then add floor? But p+small changes v for all small; maybe easier to compute log. Adding log O(n) cheap vs FFT; okay.
       # FFT
       P = center_fft(p, M)
       q = inv_fft(P*P, M)[:N]
       q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
       # If q.sum not close, normalize for weights? Use qn=q.copy; qsum=qn.sum(); if qsum>0: qn /= qsum else break
       qsum = q.sum()
       if qsum <= 0: break
       # for score use original max q? If q normalized, score of normalized same within. Let's use qn for score? Since p sum1, qsum=1; if due floor small, okay. If we normalize qn, score maybe slightly lower if qsum>1; but true p sum after floor normalized to 1 so qsum should 1. Use qn for both.
       q = qn
       qmax = float(q.max())
       sc = 2.0*n*qmax
       if sc < best_score:
          best_score=sc; best_p=p.copy(); if time to print...
       # weights
       if mode == 'topk':
          k_cur = int(round(K + (K_end-K)*frac)) if K_end is not None else int(K)
          k_cur = max(1, min(k_cur, N))
          if k_cur <= 1:
             idx = int(np.argmax(q))
             s_k = np.zeros(N); val=q[idx]; if val>0: s_k[idx]=1.0/val
          else:
             part = np.argpartition(q, N-k_cur)[-k_cur:]
             thresh = q[part].min()
             active = q >= thresh  # and q>0
             cnt = int(active.sum())
             if cnt == 0:
                active = np.zeros(N,bool); active[part]=True; cnt=k_cur
             s_k = np.zeros(N)
             q_act = q[active]
             # to avoid zero divide
             mask_act = active & (q_act > 1e-300)
             if mask_act.sum() < cnt:
                 # fallback use qmax for zero
             s_k[mask_act] = 1.0/(cnt * q[mask_act])
             # If some active zero, ignore; normalization slightly off; can renormalize s? We need w sum=1: sum(s_k*q)=sum w = for mask_act maybe <1. Renormalize s_k by total_w = np.dot(s_k,q) to 1.
             tw = float(np.dot(s_k, q))
             if tw > 0: s_k /= tw
       else: # lse
          beta_cur = float(beta + (float(beta_end)-float(beta))*frac) if beta_end is not None else float(beta)
          beta_cur = max(1.5, beta_cur)
          q_safe = np.maximum(q, 1e-300)
          lq = np.log(q_safe)
          lmax = float(lq.max())
          z = beta_cur * (lq - lmax)
          # To avoid overflow not needed, but for beta small? z<=0.
          ez = exp(z)
          # if all ez zero? max z=0 at max -> majority 1 if log max finite. But if qmax underflow to 0? q positive. ez[imax]=1.
          zsum = float(ez.sum())
          if zsum <= 0: break
          s_k = ez / (zsum * q_safe)
          # optionally set nonfinite 0
          s_k[~np.isfinite(s_k)] = 0.0
          # Ensure weights sum 1: np.dot(s_k,q) = 1/Z? Actually w=ez/Z, s=w/q. dot(s,q)=1 exactly if finite. If zeros okay.
       # cross
       srev = s_k[::-1]
       S = center_fft(srev, M)
       c = inv_fft(P * S, M)
       H = c[N-n:N][::-1]
       H = np.where(np.isfinite(H), H, 0.0)
       # H negative tiny: max 0
       H = np.maximum(H, 0.0)
       gl = 2.0 * p * H
       gl = np.where(np.isfinite(gl), gl, 0.0)
       dotg = float(np.dot(p, gl))
       grad = gl - p * dotg
       grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
       gr_norm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
       if gr_norm <= 1e-18:
          # maybe break or reduce
          break
       t += 1
       # Adam
       m = b1*m + (1.0-b1)*grad
       v2 = b2*v2 + (1.0-b2)*grad*grad
       bc1 = 1.0 - b1**t
       bc2 = 1.0 - b2**t
       step = lr * m / bc1 / (np.sqrt(v2/bc2) + eps)
       snorm = float(np.linalg.norm(step))
       if snorm > max_norm:
           step *= max_norm / snorm
       v = v - step
       # recenter to avoid drift
       v = v - v.mean()
       # optional if v range too large, renormalize from best? 
       if it % 2000 == 0? print
    return best_p,best_score
```

```
def _perturb_p(p, kind, rng, noise_scale=0.03):
  n=len(p)
  if kind==0: q=p*(1+noise_scale*rng.standard_normal(n))
  elif kind==1: q=p[::-1]*(1+...)
  elif kind==2: shift= int(rng.integers(1,n)); q=np.roll(p, shift)
  elif kind==3: # rerandomize via log normal with parent as prior: v=log(p)+0.1 normal
  elif kind==4: # random mask zero some? not
  return _normalize_p(q)
```

Let's write functions:

`def _pow2_ge(x):`
```
def _pow2_ge(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()
```

`def _safe_normalize(p, floor=0.0):`
```
def _safe_normalize(p, floor=0.0):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    p[p < 0] = 0.0
    p[p > 1000.0] = 1000.0 # maybe not necessary
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(p.size, 1.0 / p.size, dtype=np.float64)
    p = p / s
    if floor > 0.0:
        p = p + floor
        p = p / p.sum()
    return p
```

`def _score_p(p):`
```
def _score_p(p):
    p = _safe_normalize(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return float('inf')
    M = _pow2_ge(2 * n - 1)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, M)
    q = np.fft.irfft(P * P, M)[:2 * n - 1]
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    qsum = float(q.sum())
    if qsum > 0.0:
        q = q / qsum
    return float(2.0 * n * np.max(q))
```
But normalizing q changes score if p sum not 1 (it is). Good. If qsum zero, return inf.

`def _init_parent(p):` maybe.

`def _interp_to_n(p, n):`
```
def _interp_to_n(p, n):
    x_old = np.linspace(0, 1, p.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0, 1, n)
    return _safe_normalize(np.interp(x_new, x_old, p))
```

`def _make_inits(n, parent, rng, count=8):` returns list. Use parent if not None.
```
def _make_inits(n, parent, rng, count=8):
  inits=[]
  if parent is not None and parent.size>0:
     if parent.size == n: inits.append(_safe_normalize(parent))
     else: inits.append(_interp_to_n(parent,n))
     base = ... parent interpolated
     inits.append(_safe_normalize(base*(1.0+0.05*rng.standard_normal(n))))
  x=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
  inits.append(_safe_normalize(np.ones(n)))
  for f, amp, ph in [(1,0.5,0),(2,0.4,0.5),(3,0.3,0.1),(0.5,0.6,1.0)]:
      p=1.0+amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x+ph)
      inits.append(_safe_normalize(np.maximum(p,1e-6)))
  inits.append(_safe_normalize(0.3+1.0*(2*x-1)**2))
  inits.append(_safe_normalize(rng.exponential(1.0,n)))
  s=rng.choice(np.array([-1.0,1.0]), size=n)
  inits.append(_safe_normalize(1.0+0.8*s))
  v=rng.standard_normal(n)*0.5
  inits.append(_safe_normalize(np.exp(v)))
  # limit count
  return inits[:count]
```
But `rng.choice` with array? okay. To avoid imports? numpy. For no parent and many inits, perhaps too slow but okay.

`def _run_optimizer(p0, phase_deadline, mode='lse', beta=16.0, beta_end=None, K=64, K_end=None, lr=0.2, plateau_seconds=120.0, max_iter=100000000, label='', print_sec=20.0):`
Need no nested functions. We'll use `time.monotonic()` inside.
Potential issue: `phase_deadline` maybe less than start if remaining small; check.

Detailed implementation inside:
```
def _run_optimizer(...):
    n = int(np.size(p0))
    if n < 2:
       p0 = _safe_normalize(p0); return p0, _score_p(p0)
    N=2*n-1
    M=_pow2_ge(3*n-1) # safe >=3n-2
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    deadline=max(phase_deadline, t_start+1.0)
    p = _safe_normalize(p0, floor=0.0)
    # ensure positive for log
    tiny=1e-300
    p = np.maximum(p, tiny)
    psi = p / p.sum()
    v = np.log(psi)
    # center/scale v
    v = v - float(np.median(v))
    vptp = float(np.max(v)-np.min(v))
    if vptp > 700.0 and vptp>0:
         v = (v - float(np.mean(v))) * (700.0/vptp)
    # set zeros? v finite
    m = np.zeros(n); v2=np.zeros(n)
    best_score = _score_p(psi) ? expensive. We can compute first inside loop; initialize inf and last improve t_start.
    best_score = float('inf')
    best_p = psi.copy()
    last_improve = t_start
    last_print = t_start
    b1=0.9;b2=0.999;eps=1e-8
    rfft=np.fft.rfft; irfft=np.fft.irfft; exp=np.exp
    for it in range(max_iter):
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now >= deadline: break
       if now - last_improve > plateau_seconds: break
       if beta_end is not None:
          total_time=max(1.0, deadline - t_start)
          frac=min(1.0, max(0.0, (now - t_start)/total_time))
       else: frac=0.0
       # simplex
       vm=float(np.max(v))
       u=exp(v-vm)
       s=float(np.sum(u))
       if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
           break
       p=u/s
       # FFT
       P = rfft(p, M)
       q = irfft(P * P, M)
       q = q[:N].copy()
       q[~np.isfinite(q)] = 0.0
       np.maximum(q, 0.0, out=q)
       qsum=float(np.sum(q))
       if qsum <= 0.0:
           break
       if abs(qsum - 1.0) > 1e-8:
           q = q / qsum
       qmax=float(np.max(q))
       if qmax <= 0: break
       sc = 2.0*n*qmax
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
           best_score = sc
           best_p = p.copy()
           last_improve = now
           if now - last_print > max(5.0, print_sec):
               print(f"    [{label}] it={it} score={sc:.8f} beta/K={...}")
               last_print=now
       # weights
       if mode == 'topk':
           if K_end is not None:
               k_cur = int(round(float(K) + (float(K_end)-float(K))*frac))
           else: k_cur=int(K)
           k_cur=max(1,min(k_cur,N))
           s_k = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
           if k_cur == 1:
               idx=int(np.argmax(q))
               if q[idx] > 1e-300:
                   s_k[idx] = 1.0 / q[idx]
           else:
               part = np.argpartition(q, N - k_cur)
               part = part[-k_cur:]
               thresh = float(q[part].min())
               active = q >= thresh
               cnt = int(active.sum())
               if cnt == 0:
                   active[idx?]? # use part
                   active = np.zeros(N, dtype=bool); active[part]=True; cnt=k_cur
               active_q = q[active]
               ok = active_q > 1e-300
               if ok.sum() == 0:
                   # use max
                   active2 = np.zeros(N,bool); idx=int(np.argmax(q)); active2[idx]=True; cnt=1
               else:
                   active2 = active & ok
                   cnt = int(active2.sum())
               if cnt > 0:
                   s_k[active2] = 1.0 / (float(cnt) * q[active2])
                   tw = float(np.dot(s_k, q))
                   if tw > 0: s_k /= tw
       else:
           beta_cur = float(beta)
           if beta_end is not None:
               beta_cur = float(beta) + (float(beta_end)-float(beta))*frac
           if beta_cur < 1.05: beta_cur=1.05
           q_safe = np.maximum(q, 1e-300)
           lq = np.log(q_safe)
           lmax = float(np.max(lq))
           z = beta_cur * (lq - lmax)
           ez = exp(z)
           ez[~np.isfinite(ez)] = 0.0
           zsum = float(ez.sum())
           if zsum <= 0: break
           s_k = ez / (zsum * q_safe)
           s_k[~np.isfinite(s_k)] = 0.0
           # If due floor some weights where q=0 set? q_safe floor may make s huge if ez nonzero for q near zero. But ez for very small q likely zero because z very negative; if beta small, maybe not? beta>=1.05, if q ratio min 1e-300, z huge negative => zero. okay.
           # Renormalize to ensure weights sum 1? dot(s_k,q) should 1 except zeros.
           tw = float(np.dot(s_k, q))
           if tw > 0 and abs(tw-1.0)>1e-8:
               s_k = s_k / tw
       # cross
       srev = s_k[::-1]
       S = rfft(srev, M)
       conv = irfft(P * S, M)
       H = conv[N - n : N]
       # H from reversed? earlier H_i = c[N-1-i], so H = conv[N-n:N][::-1]
       H = H[::-1].copy()
       H[~np.isfinite(H)] = 0.0
       np.maximum(H, 0.0, out=H)
       gl = 2.0 * p * H
       gl[~np.isfinite(gl)] = 0.0
       dotg = float(np.dot(p, gl))
       grad = gl - p * dotg
       grad[~np.isfinite(grad)] = 0.0
       # If norm of grad zero, small random? break?
       gnorm=float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
       if gnorm <= 1e-16:
           # break to avoid waste; maybe add small noise to v?
           if it < 100: # add noise?
               v += 1e-3 * np.random.standard_normal(n)? But no rng here. Could use np.random. But top-level maybe.
           break
       t += 1? Need define t=0 before loop. Use `step_count`.
       # Adam
       m = b1*m + (1.0-b1)*grad
       v2 = b2*v2 + (1.0-b2)*(grad*grad)
       bc1 = 1.0 - b1**t
       bc2 = 1.0 - b2**t
       step = lr * m / bc1 / (np.sqrt(v2 / bc2) + eps)
       # Clip step abs to maybe 2*lr? To avoid blow
       smax = float(np.max(np.abs(step)))
       if smax > 1.0:
           step = step / smax
       v = v - step
       v = v - float(np.mean(v))
       vptp = float(np.max(v) - np.min(v))
       if vptp > 700.0 and vptp > 0.0:
           v = (v - float(np.mean(v))) * (700.0 / vptp)
    # final check
    if not np.isfinite(best_score) or best_score > 2.0:
        best_p = _safe_normalize(best_p)
    return best_p, float(best_score)
```
Need `t` variable initialized before loop (`step_count=0`). In grad norm check, if not enough small? We can break. But if grad zero at uniform, break early; okay if starting uniform. But random shapes not exactly uniform may have nonzero.

But at early iterations, q from p; if active topK symmetric and p uniform, break. For nonuniform, gnorm >0.

Concern: LSE with beta close to 1 can give gradient zero? For any p? If β=1, weights w=q, s constant, H constant? H_i = (1/N)∑ p_{k-i} not constant for edges but if p dense maybe. Could be. We set min beta 1.05 but near degenerate. Use beta_start at least 4.

Potential issue: The gradient `gl = 2*p*H` for topK uniform active with q varied is gradient of average log q. If active set includes k where q=0? We skip. Good.

But if active set topK and p uniform, H constant only if active window covers full for all i; if K small and active centered at center, for edge i, fewer active k with k-i valid, H smaller, so gradient not zero! Wait for uniform p and active topK near center: For i near 0, k-i valid for k in 0..n-1, active k around center far >n? active k<n? center ±K/2; k-i valid for i=0 all active, sum 1/q; for i=n-1, active k-i valid if k≥n-1, many active (center ±) maybe all? If K < n, for i near edges, still? Let's N=2n-1, active k around n-1 ±K/2. For i=0, active all valid. For i=n-1, valid k from n-1 to 2n-2; includes active k>=n-1 (about half). So H smaller half; gradient nonzero. So uniform not stationary for K<2n. But earlier I assumed full range; actually q triangular active centered, edges see fewer active coefficients. TopK gradient can move mass to edges. Good. LSE with weights over all coefficients (s=w/q) also H_i=∑ all k w_k p_{k-i}/q_k; for uniform at edges missing many k, H not constant. However if β close to 1, w∝q and s constant, H_i = avg p over valid window = (valid count)/n, edges half, gradient nonzero. Wait β=1 ψ constant? For log LSE with β=1, gradient should be zero. Now, top-level `_perturb_p`:
```
def _perturb_p(p, kind, rng, scale=0.03):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=p.size
    if kind == 0:
       q = p * (1.0 + scale * rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif kind == 1:
       q = p[::-1] * (1.0 + scale * rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif kind == 2:
       shift = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n)))
       q = np.roll(p, shift)
    elif kind == 3:
       v = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300)) + 0.05 * rng.standard_normal(n)
       q = np.exp(v - np.max(v))
    elif kind == 4:
       # low-rank sinusoidal modulation
       x = np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
       f = 0.5 + 8.0*rng.random()
       ph = 2*np.pi*rng.random()
       a = 0.02 + 0.05*rng.random()
       q = p * (1.0 + a*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x+ph))
    else:
       q = p.copy()
    q = _safe_normalize(q)
    return q
```
Ensure no overflow in exp kind3: v may large; subtract max.

`_try_trim`:
```
def _try_trim(p, max_trim=600, n_trials=80):
    p = _safe_normalize(p)
    n=p.size
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_sc = _score_p(p)
    if n < 50: return best_p,best_sc
    max_t = min(max_trim, n//5)
    cum_left = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(p[:max_t])))
    cum_right = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(p[-max_t:][::-1])))
    candidates=set([(0,0)])
    # grid
    for l in [0,1,2,5,10,20,50,100,200,500]:
       if l<=max_t:
          for r in [0,1,2,5,10,20,50,100,200,500]:
             if r<=max_t and l+r < n-20:
                candidates.add((l,r))
    # random pairs with small edge mass
    rng = np.random.default_rng(987)
    for _ in range(n_trials):
       l=int(rng.integers(0,max_t+1)); r=int(rng.integers(0,max_t+1)); candidates.add((l,r))
    for l,r in candidates:
       if l+r >= n-20: continue
       q = p[l:n-r]
       sc = _score_p(q)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
          best_sc=sc; best_p=q.copy()
    return best_p,best_sc
```
This may call score many times; 100*14k FFT ~ maybe 1s. Good. But if total sequence from best length 6962 and best already optimized, trim may not improve but okay. Let's code `_run_optimizer` fully with care.

Pseudo final:
```
def _run_optimizer(p0, phase_deadline, mode='lse', beta=16.0, beta_end=None, K=64, K_end=None, lr=0.2, plateau_seconds=120.0, max_iter=100000000, label=''):
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    if phase_deadline <= t_start:
        phase_deadline = t_start + 0.1
    n=int(np.size(p0))
    if n < 2:
       p0 = _safe_normalize(p0)
       return p0, _score_p(p0)
    N=2*n-1
    M=_pow2_ge(3*n - 1)
    p = _safe_normalize(p0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
    p = p / p.sum()
    v = np.log(p)
    v = v - float(np.mean(v))
    vptp = float(np.max(v)-np.min(v))
    if vptp > 700.0:
       v = v * (700.0/vptp)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_v = v.copy()
    best_score = _score_p(p)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score): best_score = 1e100
    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v2 = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    bias1=1.0; bias2=1.0
    b1=0.9; b2=0.999; eps=1e-8
    last_improve = t_start
    last_print = t_start
    print_sec = max(10.0, plateau_seconds*0.15)
    bad_streak = 0
    rfft = np.fft.rfft; irfft = np.fft.irfft; exp = np.exp
    for it in range(max_iter):
       now = time.monotonic()
       if now >= phase_deadline: break
       if now - last_improve > plateau_seconds: break
       # reset if bad
       if bad_streak > 5000:
           v = best_v.copy(); m.fill(0.0); v2.fill(0.0); bias1=1.0; bias2=1.0; bad_streak=0
           continue
       # frac
       total_dur = max(1.0, phase_deadline - t_start)
       frac = (now - t_start) / total_dur
       if frac < 0: frac=0.0
       if frac > 1: frac=1.0
       # softmax
       vm = float(np.max(v))
       u = exp(v - vm)
       ssum = float(np.sum(u))
       if not np.isfinite(ssum) or ssum <= 0.0:
           break
       p = u / ssum
       P = rfft(p, M)
       q = irfft(P * P, M)
       q = q[:N].copy()
       q[~np.isfinite(q)] = 0.0
       np.maximum(q, 0.0, out=q)
       qsum = float(np.sum(q))
       if qsum <= 0.0: break
       if abs(qsum - 1.0) > 1e-7:
           q = q / qsum
       qmax = float(np.max(q))
       if qmax <= 0.0: break
       sc = 2.0 * n * qmax
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
           best_score = sc
           best_p = p.copy()
           best_v = v.copy()
           last_improve = now
           bad_streak = 0
           if now - last_print > print_sec:
               print(f"    [{label}] NEW score={sc:.8f} t={now-t_start:.0f}s")
               last_print = now
       else:
           if sc > best_score * 1.02:
               bad_streak += 1
           else:
               bad_streak = 0
       # weights/H
       use_direct = False
       idxs = None; coeffs=None
       if mode == 'topk':
           if K_end is not None:
               k_cur = int(round(float(K) + (float(K_end)-float(K))*frac))
           else:
               k_cur = int(K)
           k_cur = max(1, min(k_cur, N))
           if k_cur == 1:
               idx = int(np.argmax(q))
               qv = float(q[idx])
               if qv <= 1e-300: qv = 1e-300
               idxs = np.array([idx], dtype=np.int64)
               coeffs = np.array([1.0/qv], dtype=np.float64)
               use_direct = True
           else:
               part = np.argpartition(q, N - k_cur)
               part = part[-k_cur:]
               thresh = float(q[part].min())
               tie = q >= thresh
               cnt_tie = int(np.sum(tie))
               if cnt_tie <= max(64, 3*k_cur):
                   cand = np.nonzero(tie)[0]
               else:
                   cand = part.copy()
               q_cand = q[cand]
               ok = q_cand > 1e-300
               if int(np.sum(ok)) == 0:
                   idx = int(np.argmax(q)); qv=float(q[idx])
                   if qv <= 1e-300: qv=1e-300
                   idxs=np.array([idx],dtype=np.int64); coeffs=np.array([1.0/qv]); use_direct=True
               else:
                   idxs = cand[ok]
                   qidx = q_cand[ok]
                   cnt = int(idxs.size)
                   coeffs = 1.0 / (float(cnt) * qidx)
                   if cnt <= 32:
                       use_direct = True
                   else:
                       s_k = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
                       s_k[idxs] = coeffs
                       tw = float(np.dot(s_k, q))
                       if tw > 0.0:
                           s_k = s_k / tw
                       else:
                           break
                       srev = s_k[::-1]
                       S = rfft(srev, M)
                       conv = irfft(P * S, M)
                       H = conv[N-n:N][::-1].copy()
                       H[~np.isfinite(H)] = 0.0
                       np.maximum(H, 0.0, out=H)
       else:
           beta_cur = float(beta)
           if beta_end is not None:
              beta_cur = float(beta) + (float(beta_end)-float(beta))*frac
           if beta_cur < 1.05: beta_cur = 1.05
           q_safe = np.maximum(q, 1e-300)
           lq = np.log(q_safe)
           lmax = float(np.max(lq))
           z = beta_cur * (lq - lmax)
           ez = exp(z)
           ez[~np.isfinite(ez)] = 0.0
           zsum = float(np.sum(ez))
           if zsum <= 0.0: break
           s_k = ez / (zsum * q_safe)
           s_k[~np.isfinite(s_k)] = 0.0
           tw = float(np.dot(s_k, q))
           if tw <= 0.0:
               break
           if abs(tw - 1.0) > 1e-7:
               s_k = s_k / tw
           srev = s_k[::-1]
           S = rfft(srev, M)
           conv = irfft(P * S, M)
           H = conv[N-n:N][::-1].copy()
           H[~np.isfinite(H)] = 0.0
           np.maximum(H, 0.0, out=H)
       if use_direct:
           H = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
           for ii in range(idxs.size):
               idx = int(idxs[ii])
               coeff = float(coeffs[ii])
               start = idx - (n - 1)
               if start < 0:
                   start = 0
               end = idx
               if end > n - 1:
                   end = n - 1
               if end >= start:
                   stop = idx - end - 1
                   if stop < 0:
                       seg = p[idx - start::-1]
                   else:
                       seg = p[idx - start:stop:-1]
                   H[start:end+1] += coeff * seg
           H[~np.isfinite(H)] = 0.0
           np.maximum(H, 0.0, out=H)
       gl = 2.0 * p * H
       gl[~np.isfinite(gl)] = 0.0
       dotg = float(np.dot(p, gl))
       grad = gl - p * dotg
       grad[~np.isfinite(grad)] = 0.0
       gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
       if gnorm <= 1e-15:
           # if at very start with uniform? break or add tiny nondeterministic? 
           if it < 10:
               v = v + 1e-3 * np.random.standard_normal(n)
               continue
           break
       m = b1*m + (1.0-b1)*grad
       v2 = b2*v2 + (1.0-b2)*(grad*grad)
       bias1 *= b1
       bias2 *= b2
       denom1 = 1.0 - bias1
       denom2 = 1.0 - bias2
       if denom1 <= 0.0 or denominator? (always >0)
       step = lr * (m / denom1) / (np.sqrt(v2 / denom2) + eps)
       step[~np.isfinite(step)] = 0.0
       smax = float(np.max(np.abs(step)))
       if smax > 1.0 and smax > 0.0:
           step = step / smax
       v = v - step
       v = v - float(np.mean(v))
       vptp = float(np.max(v) - np.min(v))
       if vptp > 700.0 and vptp > 0.0:
           v = (v - float(np.mean(v))) * (700.0 / vptp)
    return best_p, float(best_score)
```

```
def _better_update(best_p, best_score, p, sc):

    return best_p, best_score
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(self-convolution)/(sum)^2.

The core optimizer treats the heights as a probability vector p (sum=1).
It minimizes a smoothed approximation to max_k log((p*p)_k) using
Riemann-Adam on logit variables v with p = softmax(v).  The gradient is
computed with FFTs for the self-convolution and a matched cross-convolution
for the active/smoothed coefficient weights.  Top-k mode uses direct O(k*n)
slices for small active sets, which makes final peak-polishing fast.

The program refines any provided height_sequence_1 parent, explores nearby
lengths, adds perturbed restarts, and finally trims low-mass edges if that
improves the score.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def _pow2_ge(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def _safe_normalize(p, floor=0.0):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    p[p < 0.0] = 0.0
    p[p > 1000.0] = 1000.0
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(p.size, 1.0 / p.size, dtype=np.float64)
    p = p / s
    if floor > 0.0:
        p = p + floor
        p = p / p.sum()
    return p


def _score_p(p):
    p = _safe_normalize(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    M = _pow2_ge(2 * n - 1)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, M)
    q = np.fft.irfft(P * P, M)[:2 * n - 1]
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    qsum = float(q.sum())
    if qsum > 0.0:
        q = q / qsum
    return float(2.0 * n * np.max(q))


def _interp_to_n(p, n):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = max(1, int(n))
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    if p.size == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(p[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, p.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return _safe_normalize(np.interp(x_new, x_old, p))


def _make_inits(n, parent, rng, count=8):
    inits = []
    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        base = _interp_to_n(parent, n)
        inits.append(base)
        inits.append(_safe_normalize(base[::-1]))
        inits.append(_safe_normalize(base * (1.0 + 0.05 * rng.standard_normal(n))))

    if n <= 0:
        return inits

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    inits.append(_safe_normalize(np.ones(n)))

    for f, amp, ph in ((1.0, 0.5, 0.0), (2.0, 0.4, 0.7),
                       (3.0, 0.3, 1.2), (0.5, 0.6, 2.1)):
        p = 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + ph)
        inits.append(_safe_normalize(np.maximum(p, 1e-6)))

    inits.append(_safe_normalize(0.2 + 1.0 * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2))
    inits.append(_safe_normalize(rng.exponential(1.0, size=n)))

    s = np.where(rng.random(n) < 0.5, -1.0, 1.0)
    inits.append(_safe_normalize(1.0 + 0.8 * s))

    v = 0.5 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    inits.append(_safe_normalize(np.exp(v - np.max(v))))

    return inits[:count]


def _perturb_p(p, kind, rng, scale=0.03):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return _safe_normalize(p)

    if kind == 0:
        q = p * (1.0 + scale * rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif kind == 1:
        q = p[::-1] * (1.0 + scale * rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif kind == 2:
        shift = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n)))
        q = np.roll(p, shift)
    elif kind == 3:
        v = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300)) + 0.05 * rng.standard_normal(n)
        q = np.exp(v - np.max(v))
    elif kind == 4:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
        f = 0.5 + 8.0 * rng.random()
        ph = 2.0 * np.pi * rng.random()
        amp = 0.02 + 0.05 * rng.random()
        q = p * (1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + ph))
    else:
        q = p.copy()

    return _safe_normalize(q)


def _better_update(best_p, best_score, p_candidate, score_candidate):
    try:
        sc = float(score_candidate)
    except Exception:
        return best_p, float(best_score)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
        return _safe_normalize(p_candidate), sc
    return best_p, float(best_score)


def _run_optimizer(p0, phase_deadline, mode="lse", beta=16.0, beta_end=None,
                   K=64, K_end=None, lr=0.2, plateau_seconds=120.0,
                   max_iter=100000000, label=""):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    if phase_deadline <= t_start:
        phase_deadline = t_start + 0.1

    n = int(np.size(np.asarray(p0).ravel()))
    p0 = _safe_normalize(p0)
    n = int(p0.size)
    if n < 2:
        return p0, _score_p(p0)

    N = 2 * n - 1
    M = _pow2_ge(3 * n - 1)

    p = np.maximum(p0, 1e-300)
    p = p / p.sum()
    v = np.log(p)
    v = v - float(np.mean(v))
    vptp = float(np.max(v) - np.min(v))
    if vptp > 700.0 and vptp > 0.0:
        v = v * (700.0 / vptp)

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_v = v.copy()
    best_score = _score_p(p)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = 1e100

    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v2 = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    bias1 = 1.0
    bias2 = 1.0
    b1 = 0.9
    b2 = 0.999
    eps = 1e-8

    last_improve = t_start
    last_print = t_start
    print_sec = max(10.0, 0.15 * float(plateau_seconds))
    bad_streak = 0

    rfft = np.fft.rfft
    irfft = np.fft.irfft
    exp = np.exp

    for it in range(max_iter):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= phase_deadline:
            break
        if now - last_improve > float(plateau_seconds):
            break

        if bad_streak > 5000:
            v = best_v.copy()
            m.fill(0.0)
            v2.fill(0.0)
            bias1 = 1.0
            bias2 = 1.0
            bad_streak = 0
            continue

        total_dur = max(1.0, float(phase_deadline - t_start))
        frac = (now - t_start) / total_dur
        if frac < 0.0:
            frac = 0.0
        elif frac > 1.0:
            frac = 1.0

        vm = float(np.max(v))
        u = exp(v - vm)
        ssum = float(np.sum(u))
        if not np.isfinite(ssum) or ssum <= 0.0:
            break
        p = u / ssum

        P = rfft(p, M)
        q = irfft(P * P, M)
        q = q[:N].copy()
        q[~np.isfinite(q)] = 0.0
        np.maximum(q, 0.0, out=q)
        qsum = float(np.sum(q))
        if qsum <= 0.0:
            break
        if abs(qsum - 1.0) > 1e-7:
            q = q / qsum

        qmax = float(np.max(q))
        if qmax <= 0.0:
            break

        sc = 2.0 * float(n) * qmax
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_p = p.copy()
            best_v = v.copy()
            last_improve = now
            bad_streak = 0
            if now - last_print > print_sec:
                print(f"    [{label}] NEW score={sc:.8f} t={now-t_start:.0f}s")
                last_print = now
        else:
            if sc > best_score * 1.02:
                bad_streak += 1
            else:
                bad_streak = 0

        H = None
        use_direct = False
        idxs = None
        coeffs = None

        if mode == "topk":
            if K_end is not None:
                k_cur = int(round(float(K) + (float(K_end) - float(K)) * frac))
            else:
                k_cur = int(K)
            k_cur = max(1, min(k_cur, N))

            if k_cur == 1:
                idx = int(np.argmax(q))
                qv = float(q[idx])
                if qv <= 1e-300:
                    qv = 1e-300
                idxs = np.array([idx], dtype=np.int64)
                coeffs = np.array([1.0 / qv], dtype=np.float64)
                use_direct = True
            else:
                part = np.argpartition(q, N - k_cur)
                part = part[-k_cur:]
                thresh = float(q[part].min())
                tie = q >= thresh
                cnt_tie = int(np.sum(tie))
                if cnt_tie <= max(64, 3 * k_cur):
                    cand = np.nonzero(tie)[0]
                else:
                    cand = part.copy()

                q_cand = q[cand]
                ok = q_cand > 1e-300
                if int(np.sum(ok)) == 0:
                    idx = int(np.argmax(q))
                    qv = float(q[idx])
                    if qv <= 1e-300:
                        qv = 1e-300
                    idxs = np.array([idx], dtype=np.int64)
                    coeffs = np.array([1.0 / qv], dtype=np.float64)
                    use_direct = True
                else:
                    idxs = cand[ok]
                    qidx = q_cand[ok]
                    cnt = int(idxs.size)
                    coeffs = 1.0 / (float(cnt) * qidx)
                    if cnt <= 16:
                        use_direct = True
                    else:
                        s_k = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
                        s_k[idxs] = coeffs
                        tw = float(np.dot(s_k, q))
                        if tw <= 0.0:
                            break
                        if abs(tw - 1.0) > 1e-7:
                            s_k = s_k / tw
                        srev = s_k[::-1]
                        S = rfft(srev, M)
                        conv = irfft(P * S, M)
                        H = conv[N - n:N][::-1].copy()
                        H[~np.isfinite(H)] = 0.0
                        np.maximum(H, 0.0, out=H)
        else:
            beta_cur = float(beta)
            if beta_end is not None:
                beta_cur = float(beta) + (float(beta_end) - float(beta)) * frac
            if beta_cur < 1.05:
                beta_cur = 1.05

            q_safe = np.maximum(q, 1e-300)
            lq = np.log(q_safe)
            lmax = float(np.max(lq))
            z = beta_cur * (lq - lmax)
            ez = exp(z)
            ez[~np.isfinite(ez)] = 0.0
            zsum = float(np.sum(ez))
            if zsum <= 0.0:
                break

            s_k = ez / (zsum * q_safe)
            s_k[~np.isfinite(s_k)] = 0.0
            tw = float(np.dot(s_k, q))
            if tw <= 0.0:
                break
            if abs(tw - 1.0) > 1e-7:
                s_k = s_k / tw

            srev = s_k[::-1]
            S = rfft(srev, M)
            conv = irfft(P * S, M)
            H = conv[N - n:N][::-1].copy()
            H[~np.isfinite(H)] = 0.0
            np.maximum(H, 0.0, out=H)

        if use_direct:
            H = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            for ii in range(idxs.size):
                idx = int(idxs[ii])
                coeff = float(coeffs[ii])

                start = idx - (n - 1)
                if start < 0:
                    start = 0
                end = idx
                if end > n - 1:
                    end = n - 1
                if end >= start:
                    stop = idx - end - 1
                    if stop < 0:
                        seg = p[idx - start::-1]
                    else:
                        seg = p[idx - start:stop:-1]
                    H[start:end + 1] += coeff * seg

            H[~np.isfinite(H)] = 0.0
            np.maximum(H, 0.0, out=H)

        if H is None:
            break

        gl = 2.0 * p * H
        gl[~np.isfinite(gl)] = 0.0
        dotg = float(np.dot(p, gl))
        grad = gl - p * dotg
        grad[~np.isfinite(grad)] = 0.0

        gnorm = float(np.sqrt(np.dot(grad, grad)))
        if gnorm <= 1e-15:
            if it < 10:
                v = v + 1e-3 * np.random.standard_normal(n)
                continue
            break

        m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * grad
        v2 = b2 * v2 + (1.0 - b2) * (grad * grad)
        bias1 *= b1
        bias2 *= b2

        denom1 = 1.0 - bias1
        denom2 = 1.0 - bias2
        if denom1 <= 0.0:
            denom1 = 1e-12
        if denom2 <= 0.0:
            denom2 = 1e-12

        step = lr * (m / denom1) / (np.sqrt(v2 / denom2) + eps)
        step[~np.isfinite(step)] = 0.0
        smax = float(np.max(np.abs(step)))
        if smax > 1.0 and smax > 0.0:
            step = step / smax

        v = v - step
        v = v - float(np.mean(v))
        vptp = float(np.max(v) - np.min(v))
        if vptp > 700.0 and vptp > 0.0:
            v = (v - float(np.mean(v))) * (700.0 / vptp)

    return best_p, float(best_score)


def _try_trim(p, max_trim=600, n_trials=50):
    p = _safe_normalize(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n < 80:
        return p, _score_p(p)

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_sc = _score_p(p)
    max_t = min(int(max_trim), n // 5)
    if max_t < 1:
        return best_p, best_sc

    grid = [0, 1, 2, 5, 10, 20, 50, 100, 200, 500]
    candidates = set()
    for l in grid:
        if l <= max_t:
            for r in grid:
                if r <= max_t and l + r < n - 20:
                    candidates.add((l, r))

    rng = np.random.default_rng(987654321)
    for _ in range(n_trials):
        l = int(rng.integers(0, max_t + 1))
        r = int(rng.integers(0, max_t + 1))
        if l + r < n - 20:
            candidates.add((l, r))

    for l, r in candidates:
        if l + r >= n - 20:
            continue
        q = p[l:n - r]
        if q.size < 20:
            continue
        sc = _score_p(q)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_p = q.copy()

    return best_p, float(best_sc)


def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400):
    try:
        t_global = time.monotonic()
        budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s) - 60.0)
        hard_deadline = t_global + budget
        rng = np.random.default_rng(20240707)

        MAX_LEN = 12300

        parent = None
        try:
            pa = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if pa.size > 0:
                pa = np.nan_to_num(pa, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
                pa = np.clip(pa, 0.0, 1000.0)
                if pa.size > MAX_LEN:
                    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, pa.size)
                    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, MAX_LEN)
                    pa = np.interp(xn, xo, pa)
                if pa.sum() > 0.0:
                    parent = pa
        except Exception:
            parent = None

        if parent is not None:
            n_main = int(parent.size)
            best_p = _safe_normalize(parent)
        else:
            n_main = 8192
            best_p = _safe_normalize(np.ones(n_main))

        best_score = _score_p(best_p)
        if not np.isfinite(best_score):
            best_score = 1e100
            best_p = _safe_normalize(np.ones(n_main))

        print(f"initial length={best_p.size} score={best_score:.8f}")

        lengths = [n_main]
        cands = []
        if n_main < MAX_LEN:
            cands.append(min(MAX_LEN, int(round(n_main * 1.25 / 128.0) * 128)))
        if n_main < 12288:
            cands.append(12288)
        else:
            cands.append(int(round(n_main * 0.75 / 128.0) * 128)))
        cands.append(8192)

        for c in cands:
            c = max(512, int(c))
            if c > MAX_LEN:
                continue
            skip = False
            for l in lengths:
                if abs(c - l) <= 0.12 * max(1, l):
                    skip = True
                    break
            if not skip:
                lengths.append(c)
            if len(lengths) >= 3:
                break
        lengths = lengths[:3]

        if parent is None:
            for a0 in _make_inits(n_main, None, rng, 8):
                sc0 = _score_p(a0)
                best_p, best_score = _better_update(best_p, best_score, a0, sc0)

        main_tasks = [
            ("lse", 4.0, 32.0, 0, 0, 0.30, 0.12, 90.0),
            ("lse", 8.0, 128.0, 0, 0, 0.18, 0.10, 90.0),
            ("topk", 0.0, 0.0, 64, 16, 0.12, 0.08, 80.0),
            ("topk", 0.0, 0.0, 16, 4, 0.07, 0.08, 70.0),
            ("lse", 128.0, 2048.0, 0, 0, 0.05, 0.06, 60.0),
            ("topk", 0.0, 0.0, 4, 2, 0.03, 0.06, 60.0),
        ]

        for mode, b0, b1, k0, k1, lr, frac, plateau in main_tasks:
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now >= hard_deadline - 60:
                break
            remaining = hard_deadline - now
            if remaining < 45:
                break
            dur = frac * budget
            if dur > remaining - 20:
                dur = remaining - 20
            if dur < 20:
                dur = min(20.0, max(5.0, remaining - 5.0))

            p0 = best_p if best_p.size == n_main else _interp_to_n(best_p, n_main)
            pd = time.monotonic() + dur

            if mode == "topk":
                pnew, scnew = _run_optimizer(
                    p0, pd,
                    mode="topk",
                    beta=0.0, beta_end=0.0,
                    K=k0, K_end=k1,
                    lr=lr,
                    plateau_seconds=plateau,
                    label=f"main topk {k0}->{k1}"
                )
            else:
                pnew, scnew = _run_optimizer(
                    p0, pd,
                    mode="lse",
                    beta=b0, beta_end=b1,
                    K=0, K_end=0,
                    lr=lr,
                    plateau_seconds=plateau,
                    label=f"main lse {b0:.0f}->{b1:.0f}"
                )

            best_p, best_score = _better_update(best_p, best_score, pnew, scnew)
            print(f"after main task {mode} {b0}->{b1} {k0}->{k1}: {best_score:.8f}")

        for n in lengths[1:]:
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now >= hard_deadline - 120:
                break
            remaining = hard_deadline - now
            if remaining < 120:
                break

            p0 = _interp_to_n(best_p, n)
            dur = 0.08 * budget
            if dur > remaining - 80:
                dur = remaining - 80
            if dur < 60:
                dur = min(60.0, max(20.0, remaining - 30.0))

            pnew, scnew = _run_optimizer(
                p0, time.monotonic() + dur,
                mode="lse",
                beta=16.0, beta_end=256.0,
                K=0, K_end=0,
                lr=0.15,
                plateau_seconds=60.0,
                label=f"len {n} lse"
            )
            best_p, best_score = _better_update(best_p, best_score, pnew, scnew)
            print(f"length {n} after lse: {best_score:.8f}")

            if scnew != float("inf") and scnew <= best_score * 1.0 and (hard_deadline - time.monotonic()) > 90:
                n_best = int(best_p.size)
                p1 = best_p if n_best == n else _interp_to_n(best_p, n)
                dur2 = 0.05 * budget
                if dur2 > (hard_deadline - time.monotonic()) - 60:
                    dur2 = max(20.0, (hard_deadline - time.monotonic()) - 60)
                pnew, scnew = _run_optimizer(
                    p1, time.monotonic() + dur2,
                    mode="topk",
                    beta=0.0, beta_end=0.0,
                    K=16, K_end=4,
                    lr=0.08,
                    plateau_seconds=50.0,
                    label=f"len {n} topk"
                )
                best_p, best_score = _better_update(best_p, best_score, pnew, scnew)
                print(f"length {n} after topk: {best_score:.8f}")

        for k in range(4):
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now >= hard_deadline - 50:
                break
            remaining = hard_deadline - now
            if remaining < 50:
                break
            p0 = _perturb_p(best_p, k, rng, scale=0.02 + 0.012 * (k % 3))
            dur = 0.035 * budget
            if dur > remaining - 25:
                dur = remaining - 25
            if dur < 20:
                dur = min(20.0, max(5.0, remaining - 5.0))

            pnew, scnew = _run_optimizer(
                p0, time.monotonic() + dur,
                mode="topk",
                beta=0.0, beta_end=0.0,
                K=8, K_end=2,
                lr=0.08,
                plateau_seconds=30.0,
                label=f"perturb {k}"
            )
            best_p, best_score = _better_update(best_p, best_score, pnew, scnew)

        now = time.monotonic()
        if now < hard_deadline - 80:
            dur = min(0.05 * budget, hard_deadline - now - 40)
            if dur > 30:
                pnew, scnew = _run_optimizer(
                    best_p, time.monotonic() + dur,
                    mode="topk",
                    beta=0.0, beta_end=0.0,
                    K=2, K_end=1,
                    lr=0.03,
                    plateau_seconds=45.0,
                    label="final top1"
                )
                best_p, best_score = _better_update(best_p, best_score, pnew, scnew)

        now = time.monotonic()
        if now < hard_deadline - 60:
            dur = min(0.04 * budget, hard_deadline - now - 30)
            if dur > 30:
                pnew, scnew = _run_optimizer(
                    best_p, time.monotonic() + dur,
                    mode="lse",
                    beta=512.0, beta_end=8192.0,
                    K=0, K_end=0,
                    lr=0.03,
                    plateau_seconds=40.0,
                    label="final lse"
                )
                best_p, best_score = _better_update(best_p, best_score, pnew, scnew)

        now = time.monotonic()
        if now < hard_deadline - 180:
            dur = min(300.0, hard_deadline - now - 60)
            if dur > 60:
                pnew, scnew = _run_optimizer(
                    best_p, time.monotonic() + dur,
                    mode="topk",
                    beta=0.0, beta_end=0.0,
                    K=4, K_end=1,
                    lr=0.02,
                    plateau_seconds=120.0,
                    label="extra polish"
                )
                best_p, best_score = _better_update(best_p, best_score, pnew, scnew)

        if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 20:
            ptrim, sctrim = _try_trim(best_p)
            best_p, best_score = _better_update(best_p, best_score, ptrim, sctrim)

        out_list = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in np.asarray(best_p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()]
        if len(out_list) == 0 or sum(out_list) < 0.01:
            out_list = [1.0 / 1024.0] * 1024

        try:
            verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out_list))
            print(f"verified score={verified:.8f}")
        except Exception:
            verified = best_score

        if not np.isfinite(verified) or verified > 10.0:
            out_list = [1.0 / 1024.0] * 1024

        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_global
        print(f"FINAL internal={best_score:.8f} length={len(out_list)} time={elapsed:.1f}s")
        return out_list

    except Exception:
        try:
            fb = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
            return [float(x) for x in fb]
        except Exception:
            return [1.0 / 1024.0] * 1024
```
